[EVENT: PSE34190]
Date: 2023-06-04
Country: Palestine | Location: Burqah
Type: Riots > Mob violence | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Rioters (Israel) [Rioters]
Actor2: Rioters (Palestine) [Rioters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Dunia Al Watan; Jerusalem Post; Palestine News and Information Agency; PCHR: Palestinian Centre for Human Rights; PLO Negotiations Affairs Department; Quds News Network; Times of Israel; Twitter [Other-New media]

DESCRIPTION:
On 4 June 2023, a group of Israeli settlers from Homesh, under the protection of soldiers, attacked Palestinian houses [presumably by stone-throwing] in Burqah village (Nablus, West Bank) where riot clashes erupted between Palestinians and settlers. Soldiers fired live and rubber bullets and tear gas canisters at rioters. As a result, 4 Palestinian were injured with shrapnel, another 4 with rubber bullets, and 55 Palestinians suffered from tear gas inhalation. Also, settlers beat a Palestinian and smashed vehicles' glass. And 4 settlers were injured by stone-throwing. Israeli media said that soldiers involved in the incident to separate the two parties.
